copy cell values/styles\n    for row in src_ws.iter_rows():\n        for cell in row:\n            dst_cell = dst_ws.cell(row=cell.row, column=cell.column)\n            copy_cell(cell, dst_cell)\n    # --- Build lookup from event log ---\n    # headers\n    event_headers = [event_ws.cell(1, c).value for c in range(1, event_ws.max_column + 1)]\n    idx_fac = event_headers.index('Facility') + 1\n    idx_sess = event_headers.index('Session ID') + 1\n    idx_bin = event_headers.index('Bin ID') + 1\n    idx_type = event_headers.index('Event Type') + 1\n    idx_qty = event_headers.index('Count Qty') + 1\n\n    # iterate rows in order, build last-by-row-order dict\n    lookup = {}\n    for r in range(2, event_ws.max_row + 1):\n        fac = event_ws.cell(r, idx_fac).value\n        sess = event_ws.cell(r, idx_sess).value\n        binid = event_ws.cell(r, idx_bin).value\n        etype = event_ws.cell(r, idx_type).value\n        qty = event_ws.cell(r, idx_qty).value\n\n        # Event Type == FINAL (case-insensitive)\n        if etype is None or str(etype).strip().upper() != 'FINAL':\n            continue\n        # exclude rows where any of Facility, Session ID, Bin ID is blank/None\n        if fac is None or str(fac).strip() == '' or sess is None or str(sess).strip() == '' or binid is None or str(binid).strip() == '':\n            continue\n        # exclude rows where Count Qty is blank/None\n        if qty is None or (isinstance(qy := qty, str) and qty.strip() == ''):\n            continue\n\n        fac_s = str(fac).strip()\n        sess_s = str(sess).strip()\n        bin_s = str(binid).strip()\n        key = (fac_s, sess_s, bin_s)\n        # keep last by row order -> just overwrite\n        lookup[key] = qty\n\n    # --- Read plan rows ---\n    plan_headers = [plan_ws.cell(1, c).value for c in range(1, plan_ws.max_column + 1)]\n    plan_rows = []\n    for r in range(2, plan_ws.max_row + 1):\n        row = [plan_ws.cell(r, c).value for c in range(1, plan_ws.max_column + 1)]\n        plan_rows.append(row)\n\n    # plan indices\n    p_fac = plan_headers.index('Facility')\n    p_sess = plan_headers.index('Session ID')\n    p_bin = plan_headers.index('Bin ID')\n    p_prod = plan_headers.index('Product ID')\n    p_exp = plan_headers.index('Expected Qty')\n    p_var = plan_headers.index('Allowed Variance')\n    p_appr = plan_headers.index('Approval Needed')\n\n    # --- Build output workbook ---\n    out_wb = openpyxl.Workbook()\n    # default sheet\n    default_ws = out_wb.active\n    out_wb.remove(default_ws)\n\n    # Overview sheet (copy from template)\n    ov = out_wb.create_sheet('Overview')\n    copy_sheet(tmpl_ws, ov)\n\n    # RawData sheet (copy plan table exactly)\n    raw = out_wb.create_sheet('RawData')\n    for r in range(1, plan_ws.max_row + 1):\n        for c in range(1, plan_ws.max_column + 1):\n            raw.cell(row=r, column=c).value = plan_ws.cell(r, c).value\n\n    # Formatted Data sheet\n    fmt = out_wb.create_sheet('Formatted Data')\n    fmt_headers = ['Facility', 'Session ID', 'Bin ID', 'Product ID', 'Expected Qty',\n                   'Allowed Variance', 'Approval Needed', 'Missing Final Count',\n                   'Approval Gap', 'Total Errors', 'Error Summary']\n    for c, h in enumerate(fmt_headers, start=1):\n        fmt.cell(row=1, column=c).value = h\n\n    for i, prow in enumerate(plan_rows, start=2):\n        fac = str(prow[p_fac]).strip() if prow[p_fac] is not None else None\n        sess = str(prow[p_sess]).strip() if prow[p_sess] is not None else None\n        binid = str(prow[p_bin]).strip() if prow[p_bin] is not None else None\n        prod = prow[p_prod]\n        exp = prow[p_exp]\n        allvar = prow[p_var]\n        appr = prow[p_appr]\n\n        key = (fac, sess, binid)\n        in_lookup = key in lookup\n        missing = 0 if in_lookup else 1\n\n        appr_yes = False\n        if appr is not None and str(appr).strip().upper() == 'YES':\n            appr_yes = True\n\n        gap = 0\n        if in_lookup and appr_yes:\n            try:\n                exp_n = float(exp)\n                cnt_n = float(lookup[key])\n                allvar_n = float(allvar)\n                if abs(exp_n - cnt_n) > allvar_n:\n                    gap = 1\n            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n                gap = 0\n\n        total = missing + gap\n        parts = []\n        if missing:\n            parts.append('Missing Final Count')\n        if gap:\n            parts.append('Approval Gap')\n        err_summary = ', '.join(parts) if parts else 'None'\n\n        out_row = [fac, sess, binid, prod, exp, allvar, appr, missing, gap, total, err_summary]\n        for c, val in enumerate(out_row, start=1):\n            fmt.cell(row=i, column=c).value = val\n\n    # Summary sheet\n    summ = out_wb.create_sheet('Summary')\n    summ_headers = ['Facility', 'Session ID', 'Missing Final Counts', 'Approval Gaps', 'Total Errors']\n    for c, h in enumerate(summ_headers, start=1):\n        summ.cell(row=1, column=c).value = h\n\n    # aggregate from Formatted Data by (Facility, Session ID), include only Total Errors > 0\n    agg = {}\n    for r in range(2, fmt.max_row + 1):\n        fac = fmt.cell(r, 1).value\n        sess = fmt.cell(r, 2).value\n        miss = fmt.cell(r, 8).value or 0\n        gap = fmt.cell(r, 9).value or 0\n        tot = fmt.cell(r, 10).value or 0\n        k = (fac, sess)\n        if k not in agg:\n            agg[k] = [0, 0, 0]\n        agg[k][0] += miss\n        agg[k][1] += gap\n        agg[k][2] += tot\n\n    # include only groups where Total Errors > 0\n    groups = [(k, v) for k, v in agg.items() if v[2] > 0]\n    # sort by Facility asc, then Session ID asc\n    groups.sort(key=lambda x: (str(x[0][0]), str(x[0][1])))\n\n    g_miss = g_gap = g_tot = 0\n    r = 2\n    for (fac, sess), (m, gp, t) in groups:\n        summ.cell(row=r, column=1).value = fac\n        summ.cell(row=r, column=2).value = sess\n        summ.cell(row=r, column=3).value = m\n        summ.cell(row=r, column=4).value = gp\n        summ.cell(row=r, column=5).value = t\n        g_miss += m\n        g_gap += gp\n        g_tot += t\n        r += 1\n\n    #
